# Excalidraw Data

## Text Elements
To determine the analysis of the number of nodes in any height h in a heap, first let's see the structure  ^om0ll9nU

Indeed we can group them by height ^i8DsAy8e

Now, let's see some cases ^vvEBg376

Now, what represents this? It represents all the nodes that are valid from the index i. To know the
count of all of them, let's do the next:  ^sqz7TmkZ

Let's see for the next:  ^6UDwA0jG

From here you can generalize the pattern  ^HU0DrUDg
